			}
			}

			}
		}
	}

						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {

			}
			}																					
		}

			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_fortification_expert
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesElevenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesElevenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

		}
		}

		}

			}
		}

		name = cosmo_natrader_events.1210.a

				has_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible	
			}
			trader_wives_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoNACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = na_concubine_12c
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = traderwife_here
				culture = FROM
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}																
		set_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_natrader_events.1230.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible	
			}
			trader_wives_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoNACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_theologian
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}

			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = na_concubine_11a
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = traderwife_here
				culture = FROM
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}																
		set_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible																
		}
	}		
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_natrader_events.1510.a	   
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible	
			}
			trader_wives_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoNACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesOneCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_fortification_expert
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

			}

		}	
			}

		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesSixteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesSixteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

		   }

				}					   
			}

		which = cosmoNAWivesSeventeenACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesSeventeenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesSeventeenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesSeventeenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_natrader_events.1800
			}
			}			
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_13a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_13b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_13c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {

			}
			}																					
		}

				}

		which = cosmoNAWivesEighteenACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesEighteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesEighteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesEighteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}
